jJ(jk,jm,jw,jh,jy)local js,jc,jM,jV,jl,jf,jQ,jn,jr,jN,jC,jB,ja,jP,jW,jD=vi[0x6],vi[7],vi[8],vi[0X9],jk[1],jk[0X2],jk[0x3],jk[0x4],jk[0X5],jk[0x6],jk[0x7],jk[0b1000],jm,jw,jh,jy local local function ZH(Zd,ZU,Zx,Zq,ZT,Zo)if#Zd==0 then return Zd end Zx,Zq,ZT,Zo=Zx or 1,Zq or 0,ZT or 0,Zo or 0 local Zg,Zi={},1 while Zi<=#Zd do local ZI=jJ(ZU,Zx,Zq,ZT,Zo)for Zb=1,16,1 do local jJ(Zm,0,Zw,Zh%SL,Se(Zh/SL))end local function Zy(Zs,Zc,ZM,ZV,Zl)local Zf=Zk(Zl,ZM,ZV)local ZQ={SE(Zc,Zf[1])}for ZO=1,0X8,1 do ZQ[ZO+1]=SE(Zs[ZO],Zf[ZO+1])end ZQ[0b1010]=Zf[0b1010]return ZQ end local function Zn(Zr,ZN,ZC,ZB)local Za=Zk(ZB,ZN,ZC)return{SE(Zr,Za[1]),Za[0xa]}end local function ZP(ZW,ZD,Ze,Zu,ZK)local ZX=Zk(ZK,Ze,Zu)return ZW[0x2]==ZX[10]and ZW[1]==SE(ZD,ZX[1])end local function ZL(ZY,ZE,Zz,tA,tv)local tG=Zk(tv,Zz,tA)if ZY[10]~=tG[0Xa]or ZY[1]~=SE(ZE,tG[1])then return nil end local tR={}for tS=1,8,1 do tR[tS]=SE(ZY[tS+1],tG[tS+1])end return tR end local function tj(tZ)local tt={}for tF=1,#tZ,1 do local tH=tZ[tF]tt[tF]=SK(tH%0x100,Se(tH/0x100)%0X100,Se(tH/65536)%0X100,Se(tH/vi[10])%256)end return SX(tt)end return{block=jJ;crypt=ZH;seal=Zy,sealAdmission=Zn,admit=ZP,resolve=ZL;packWords=tj}end))()local tU={vi[11],vi[0b1100];vi[13],vi[14],vi[15];vi[16],vi[0b10001];vi[0X12]}local function tx(tq,tT,to)local tg=(vT(0x55b8c0e))local ti={}local tI=vo[vT(0x6aed2a82)][vT(0x32ab4455)]for tb=1,#tg,1 do ti[tI(tg,tb)]=tb-1 end local tp=vo[vT(0x6aed2a82)][vT(2076206163)]local tJ=vo[vT(2039232665)][vT(1033793603)]local tk=vo[vT(1793927810)][vT(0x729d1c3c)]local tm=vo[vT(0X3956f140)][vT(0X2fc3c32d)]local tw=vo[vT(0x3956f140)][vT(1897760725)]local function th(ty)return td[vT(1264160076)](ty,tT,to)end if vo[vT(0x78807013)](tq)~=(vT(0X6aed2a82))then return tq end local ts=tp(tq)local tc={}local tM=1 while tM<=ts do local tV=(ts-tM)+1 local tl=tV>=0X5 and 0x5 or tV local tf=0 local tQ=tl>1 for tO=0,0x4,1 do local tn if tO<tl then tn=ti[tI(tq,tM+tO)]if not tn then tQ=false break end else tn=0X54 end tf=tf*0x55+tn end if tQ then local tr=tJ(tf/vi[0Xa])%256 local tN=tJ(tf/65536)%256 local tC=tJ(tf/256)%256 local tB=tf%256 if tl==0x5 then tm(tc,tk(tr,tN,tC,tB))elseif tl==4 then tm(tc,tk(tr,tN,tC))elseif tl==0X3 then tm(tc,tk(tr,tN))elseif tl==2 then tm(tc,tk(tr))end end tM=tM+tl end return th(tw(tc))end local ta={}local Fo=((function(tP,tW,tD,te)local tu=tx(tD,te)local tK,tX=vo[vT(0X6aed2a82)][vT(0x32ab4455)],vo[vT(0X6aed2a82)][vT(0X2f9fc96a)]local function tL(tY,tE)local tz,FA,Fv,FG=tK(tY,tE,tE+0x3)return((tz+FA*0x100)+Fv*0X10000)+FG*vi[0xa]end local FR={}local FS,Fj=1,1 for FZ=1,#tu,0x4 do local Ft=tL(tu,FZ)local FF=Ft%0x10000 local FH=vo[vT(2039232665)][vT(0X3d9e7043)](Ft/0x10000)FR[FF]={FS,FH;Fj}FS,Fj=FS+FH,Fj+1 end tu=nil return function(Fd)local FU=FR[Fd]if not FU then return nil end local Fx=tX(tP,FU[1],(FU[1]+FU[0x2])-1)Fx=tx(Fx,tW,FU[0x3])if#Fx~=8 and#Fx~=0b101000 then return nil end local Fq={}for FT=1,#Fx/0x4,1 do Fq[FT]=tL(Fx,((FT-1))*4+1)end return Fq end
